# GSQ224 attention lifecycle and bounded cost trial

This prospective trial keeps all target weights, grouped arithmetic, two streamed drafts, expert geometry and complete lookahead reserve unchanged. It changes only the forecast tap and window through the already existing attention mechanism. The full Engine coding case, pure-function grader, ten-GB ceiling and quiet-host eligibility are reused.

V1 stops after the existing lifecycle gate, before any speed trial. Both demand and attention return immediate EOS on the unframed synthetic 260-token fixture, so the nonempty-work and primed-continuation requirements correctly fail. Token comparisons agree; no model runtime error is observed. Preserve this failed gate. V2 reuses the existing native framed fixture at identical token lengths and its generated one-token continuation. No arithmetic, golden, assertion, memory or performance threshold changes. The independent GSQ reference golden is reused, never regenerated.

V2 completes all 104 lifecycle assertions, including demand/tap equality at 44, 260 and 2054 tokens; authenticated GSQ token golden; disk/memory prefix continuation; cancellation; live shrink/recovery and saved ceiling; byte accounting; and HTTP completion. The lifecycle fixture has its existing conservative planning allowance and small explicit arenas, with an unchanged ten-GB physical watchdog. It is not an automatic deployment recommendation. Its maximum observed process footprint is recorded below.

Only then does the actual ten-GB planner-backed cost process run. The same 81-token answer passes all three function/input-preservation tests, is token-exact against boundary lookahead and has no timing exclusions. Attention passes the frozen five-percent useful-gain and latency conditions. The raw coordinator preserves committed rate, first text, complete request, physical peak and counter comparisons. This is a single pilot against a reused boundary result, not paired qualification, proof of universal quality equivalence or an unmeasured Mac's speed.

The retained research implementation admits attention only for exact GSQ224 grouped or the previously admitted native standalone path. It does not alter the original or Auto defaults. No new model weights, raw logits or paid compute are created.

Separate bounded retirement receipts preserve complete hashes and archives while removing only superseded generated executables. Original installation, all weights, current accepted binaries and canonical raw results remain.
